50:["$","p",null,{"children":["To reduce the statistical noise of the shadows, the paper uses ",["$","em",null,{"children":"common randomized measurements"}]," (CRM) ",["$","$L1d",null,{"href":"#references","children":"[5]"}],". For each setting, it subtracts the average shadow that a prior guess of the state would produce in the same setting, and then adds back the prior's exact value. The correction averages to zero, so the prior changes the size of the statistical error but not what is estimated. Here the prior is the ideal state with 10% local depolarization, close to the true 12%. On hardware, the noise level is unknown, so the hardware example fits the prior to measured purities instead, as the paper does."],"className":"group paragraph-el text-text-primary mx-0"}]
